```APIDOC
MCCoroutine:
  interface MCCoroutine
    Hidden internal MCCoroutine interface.

  Companion:
    object Companion

  Functions:
    disable(handler: Any): Unit
```

### Database Operations with Minecraft PlayerEntity

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/plugin/index.html

Handles asynchronous database operations for player data within a Minecraft server environment (e.g., Bukkit/Spigot). It uses `Dispatchers.IO` for database tasks and interacts with `PlayerEntity` for player information.

```kotlin
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import net.minecraft.entity.player.PlayerEntity
import java.util.*

class Database() {
    suspend fun createDbIfNotExist() {
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[createDbIfNotExist] Creating database on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... create tables
        }
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player: PlayerEntity) : PlayerData {
        println("[getDataFromPlayer] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        val playerData = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[getDataFromPlayer] Retrieving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... get from database by player uuid or create new playerData instance.
            PlayerData(player.uuid, player.name.toString(), Date(), Date())
        }

        println("[getDataFromPlayer] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        return playerData;
    }

    suspend fun saveData(player: PlayerEntity, playerData : PlayerData) {
        println("[saveData] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)

        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[saveData] Saving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // insert or update playerData
        }

        println("[saveData] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }
}
```

### JavaScript Theme Toggle and Storage

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.sponge/ticks.html

Handles the initial theme setting based on OS preference or local storage, and provides functionality to toggle the theme.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode");
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage);
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Async Coroutine Dispatcher (Bukkit)

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/ARTICLE.md

Implements a CoroutineDispatcher for asynchronous tasks in Bukkit. It dispatches tasks asynchronously if not on the primary thread, otherwise uses the server scheduler for asynchronous execution.

```kotlin
class AsyncCoroutineDispatcher(private val plugin: Plugin) : CoroutineDispatcher() {
    /**
     * Handles dispatching the coroutine on the correct thread.
     */
    override fun dispatch(context: CoroutineContext, block: Runnable) {
        if (Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
            plugin.server.scheduler.runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, block)
        } else {
            block.run()
        }
    }
}
```

### JavaScript Theme Toggler

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit/-event-execution-type/index.html

This script handles the dark mode toggle functionality for the documentation page. It checks local storage for user preferences and applies the 'theme-dark' class to the HTML element accordingly. It also includes logic to detect OS-level dark mode preferences if no local storage setting is found.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../\n";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

### Initialize Dark Mode and JS Class

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.sponge/-suspending-command-element/to-command-element.html

This snippet initializes dark mode based on local storage or OS preference and replaces the 'no-js' class with 'js' on the document element.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../"; document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js"); const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode") if (storage == null) { const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) { document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark") } } else { const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage) if(savedDarkMode === true) { document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark") } }
```

### JavaScript Theme Toggle and Initialization

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit/-coroutine-session/fire-suspending-event.html

This script initializes the page by replacing 'no-js' with 'js' in the document's class list. It also handles dark mode preferences based on local storage or OS settings.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode");
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage);
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
}
```

### Tabbed Content Navigation

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/faq/index.html

Handles the logic for navigating tabbed content based on URL hash fragments and stored tab states. It iterates through tab sets, finds matching labels, and checks the corresponding input elements to display the correct tab.

```javascript
var tabs=__md_get("__tabs");if(Array.isArray(tabs))e:for(var set of document.querySelectorAll(".tabbed-set")){var labels=set.querySelector(".tabbed-labels");for(var tab of tabs)for(var label of labels.getElementsByTagName("label"))if(label.innerText.trim()===tab){var input=document.getElementById(label.htmlFor);input.checked=!0;continue e}} var target=document.getElementById(location.hash.slice(1));target&&target.name&&(target.checked=target.name.startsWith("__tabbed_"))
```

### MCCoroutine Driver API

### Initialize Dark Mode Preference

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.fabric/-m-c-coroutine-exception-event/index.html

Initializes the dark mode preference based on local storage or OS preference. If no preference is found in local storage, it checks the operating system's preferred color scheme. If the OS prefers dark mode, the 'theme-dark' class is added to the HTML element.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../\n";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

### Dark Mode Initialization

This JavaScript code initializes dark mode based on user preferences or local storage settings. It checks the operating system's preferred color scheme and applies a dark theme if specified.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../\n";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
```

### Initialize Dark Mode

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit/-event-execution-type/-consecutive/index.html

Initializes dark mode based on user preference or operating system settings. It checks local storage for a saved preference and falls back to the OS preference if none is found.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../../";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

### Dispatcher Container

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/ARTICLE.md

A container object to store and provide instances of asynchronous and synchronous CoroutineDispatchers for Minecraft plugins. It lazily initializes the dispatchers using a specified plugin.

```kotlin
object DispatcherContainer {
    private var asyncCoroutine: CoroutineContext? = null
    private var syncCoroutine: CoroutineContext? = null

    /**
     * Gets the async coroutine context.
     */
    val async: CoroutineContext
        get() {
            if (asyncCoroutine == null) {
                asyncCoroutine = AsyncCoroutineDispatcher(JavaPlugin.getPlugin(YourPlugin::class.java))
            }

            return asyncCoroutine!!
        }

    /**
     * Gets the sync coroutine context.
     */
    val sync: CoroutineContext
        get() {
            if (syncCoroutine == null) {
                syncCoroutine = MinecraftCoroutineDispatcher(JavaPlugin.getPlugin(YourPlugin::class.java))
            }

            return syncCoroutine!!
        }
}
```

### Database Operations with Coroutines (Kotlin)

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/plugin/index.html

Demonstrates asynchronous database operations using Kotlin coroutines and `withContext` for thread management. It shows how to perform operations on a dedicated IO thread pool.

```kotlin
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.Player
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.*

class Database() {
    suspend fun createDbIfNotExist() {
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] Start on any thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[createDbIfNotExist] Creating database on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)
            // ... create tables
        }
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] End on velocity plugin threadpool " + Thread.currentThread().name)
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player: Player): PlayerData {
        println("[getDataFromPlayer] Start on any thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)
        val playerData = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[getDataFromPlayer] Retrieving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)
            // ... get from database by player uuid or create new playerData instance.
            PlayerData(player.uniqueId, player.username, Date(), Date())
        }

        println("[getDataFromPlayer] End on velocity plugin threadpool " + Thread.currentThread().name)
        return playerData;
    }

    suspend fun saveData(player: Player, playerData: PlayerData) {
        println("[saveData] Start on any thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)

        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[saveData] Saving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().name)
            // insert or update playerData
        }

        println("[saveData] End on velocity plugin threadpool " + Thread.currentThread().name)
    }
}
```

```kotlin
class AdminCommandExecutor: SuspendingCommandExecutor {
```

### Int to Ticks Conversion

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit/index.html

Converts an integer to Minecraft ticks for use with delay(). Note that 1 tick does not directly equal 50 milliseconds when used standalone with delay().

```kotlin
fun Int.ticks(): Long
  Description: Converts the number to ticks for being used together with delay(..).
  Example: delay(1.ticks)
  Note: Minecraft ticks 20 times per second, which means a tick appears every 50 milliseconds. However, delay() does not directly work with the BukkitScheduler and needs millisecond manipulation to work as expected. Therefore, 1 tick does not equal 50 milliseconds when using this method standalone and only sums up to 50 milliseconds if you use it together with delay.
```

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.velocity/-m-c-coroutine-exception-event/-m-c-coroutine-exception-event.html

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../\n";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

### Background Repeating Task for Cache Saving

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/caching/index.html

Extends the `DatabaseCache` class to include a background repeating task that saves cached player data every 10 minutes. It uses `plugin.launch` for coroutine scope and `delay` for scheduling, also handling player online status to remove offline players from the cache.

```kotlin
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import org.bukkit.entity.Player
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin

class DatabaseCache(private val database: Database, private val plugin: Plugin) {
    private val cache = HashMap<Player, Deferred<PlayerData>>()

    init {
        // This plugin.launch launches a new scope in the minecraft server context which can be understood
        // to be a background task and behaves in a similar way to Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, Runnable {  })
        plugin.launch {
            // This background task is a repeatable task which in this case is an endless loop. The endless loop
            // is automatically stopped by MCCoroutine once you reload your plugin.
            while (true) {
                // Save all cached player data every 10 minutes.
                for (player in cache.keys.toTypedArray()) {
                    database.saveData(player, cache[player]!!.await())

                    // Remove player when no longer online
                    if (!player.isOnline) {
                        cache.remove(player)
                    }
                }

                // Suspending the current context is important in this case otherwise the minecraft thread will only execute this
                // endless loop as it does not have time to execute other things. Delay gives the thread time to execute other things.
                delay(10 * 60 * 1000) // 10 minutes
            }
        }
    }

    fun clear() {
        cache.clear()
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player: Player): PlayerData {
        return coroutineScope {
            if (!cache.containsKey(player)) {
                // Cache miss, create a new job
                cache[player] = async(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    database.getDataFromPlayer(player)
                }
            }

            // Await suspends the current context until the value of the ``Deferred`` job is ready.
            cache[player]!!.await()
        }
    }
}
```

### Bukkit Database Operations

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/plugin/index.html

Handles asynchronous database operations for Bukkit-based Minecraft servers. It uses Kotlin coroutines to offload I/O tasks to a background thread, preventing main thread lag. The `Database` class includes functions to initialize the database, fetch player data, and persist changes.

```kotlin
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.bukkit.entity.Player
import java.util.*

class Database() {
    suspend fun createDbIfNotExist() {
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] Start on the caller thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[createDbIfNotExist] Creating database on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... create tables
        }
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] End on the caller thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player : Player) : PlayerData {
        println("[getDataFromPlayer] Start on the caller thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        val playerData = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[getDataFromPlayer] Retrieving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... get from database by player uuid or create new playerData instance.
            PlayerData(player.uniqueId, player.name, Date(), Date())
        }

        println("[getDataFromPlayer] End on the caller thread  " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        return playerData;
    }

    suspend fun saveData(player : Player, playerData : PlayerData) {
        println("[saveData] Start on the caller thread  " + Thread.currentThread().id)

        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[saveData] Saving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // insert or update playerData
        }

        println("[saveData] End on the caller thread  " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }
}
```

```kotlin
         * This switches MCCoroutine to the test implementation of MCCoroutine.
         * It affects all the tests in the current session.
         */
        MCCoroutine.Driver = TestMCCoroutine.Driver
    }
    
    @Test
    fun testCase01(){
    }
}
```

### SuspendingSimpleCommand API

Defines the interface for suspending commands, including execution, permission checks, and suggestions.

```APIDOC
SuspendingSimpleCommand:
  execute(invocation: Any): suspend Unit
    Description: Executes the command for the specified invocation.
    Parameters:
      invocation: The invocation context for the command.
    Returns: A suspending Unit.

  hasPermission(invocation: Any): Boolean
    Description: Tests to check if the source has permission to perform the specified invocation.
    Parameters:
      invocation: The invocation context to check permissions against.
    Returns: True if the source has permission, false otherwise.

  suggest(invocation: Any): suspend List<String>
    Description: Provides tab complete suggestions for the specified invocation.
    Parameters:
      invocation: The invocation context for which to provide suggestions.
    Returns: A list of suggestion strings.
```

### Minestom Database Operations

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/plugin/index.html

Implements asynchronous database operations for Minestom-based Minecraft servers. This version of the `Database` class utilizes Kotlin coroutines for non-blocking I/O, ensuring the game's performance is not impacted. It includes methods for database initialization, retrieving player data using Minestom's `Player` object, and saving data.

```kotlin
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import net.minestom.server.entity.Player
import java.util.*

class Database() {
    suspend fun createDbIfNotExist() {
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[createDbIfNotExist] Creating database on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... create tables
        }
        println("[createDbIfNotExist] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player : Player) : PlayerData {
        println("[getDataFromPlayer] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        val playerData = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            println("[getDataFromPlayer] Retrieving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // ... get from database by player uuid or create new playerData instance.
            PlayerData(player.uuid, player.username, Date(), Date())
        }

        println("[getDataFromPlayer] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
        return playerData;
    }

    suspend fun saveData(player : Player, playerData : PlayerData) {
        println("[saveData] Start on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)

        withContext(Dispatchers.IO){
            println("[saveData] Saving player data on database io thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
            // insert or update playerData
        }

        println("[saveData] End on minecraft thread " + Thread.currentThread().id)
    }
}
```

### MCCoroutine Bungeecord API

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bungeecord/index.html

Provides core functionalities for managing coroutines within a BungeeCord plugin. Includes launching coroutines, registering suspending commands and listeners, and accessing the coroutine scope and dispatcher.

```APIDOC
Package: com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bungeecord

Types:
- CoroutineSession: Facade of a coroutine session of a single plugin.
- MCCoroutine: Interface for MCCoroutine functionality.
- MCCoroutineExceptionEvent: A BungeeCord event called when an exception is raised in MCCoroutine-managed coroutines. Cancelling this event prevents logging and allows custom handling.
- SuspendingCommand: Abstract class for commands that can suspend.
- SuspendingPlugin: Open class for plugins that utilize suspending functions.

Properties:
- bungeeCordDispatcher: Gets the plugin bungeeCord dispatcher. Type: CoroutineContext
- scope: Gets the plugin coroutine scope. Type: Unknown Class

Functions:
- launch: Launches a new coroutine on the BungeeCord Plugin ThreadPool without blocking the current thread. Returns a Job reference. Can specify context, start mode, and a suspending block.
  Signature: launch(context: CoroutineContext = bungeeCordDispatcher, start: CoroutineStart = CoroutineStart.DEFAULT, block: suspend UnknownClass.() -> Unit): UnknownClass
- registerSuspendingCommand: Registers a command executor with suspending functions. Equivalent to PluginManager.registerCommand.
  Signature: registerSuspendingCommand(plugin: UnknownClass, command: SuspendingCommand)
- registerSuspendingListener: Registers an event listener with suspending functions. Equivalent to PluginManager.registerListener but enables suspension functions. Example usage is provided.
  Signature: registerSuspendingListener(plugin: UnknownClass, listener: UnknownClass)
```

--------------------------------

### Async Task Execution

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/ARTICLE.md

Executes a given function asynchronously using the provided plugin container. Supports optional delays and repeating intervals.

```kotlin
/**
 * Executes the given [f] via the [plugin] asynchronous.
 */
fun Any.async(plugin: PluginContainer, delayTicks: Long = 0L, repeatingTicks: Long = 0L, f: () -> Unit) {
    val builder = Task.builder().async().execute(f).delayTicks(delayTicks)

    if (repeatingTicks > 0) {
        builder.intervalTicks(repeatingTicks)
    }

    builder.submit(plugin)
}
```

### Initialize Dark Mode and JS Class

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.minestom/-m-c-coroutine/disable.html

Initializes dark mode based on local storage or OS preference and replaces the 'no-js' class with 'js' on the document element.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../\n";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

### Tabbed Content Handling

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/404.html

This JavaScript code snippet manages tabbed content within the documentation. It retrieves tab states from local storage and applies them to the corresponding tabbed sections on the page, ensuring that the correct tab is displayed when the page loads.

```javascript
var tabs = __md_get("__tabs");
if (Array.isArray(tabs)) {
  e: for (var set of document.querySelectorAll(".tabbed-set")) {
    var labels = set.querySelector(".tabbed-labels");
    for (var tab of tabs) {
      for (var label of labels.getElementsByTagName("label")) {
        if (label.innerText.trim() === tab) {
          var input = document.getElementById(label.htmlFor);
          input.checked = !0;
          continue e;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
var target = document.getElementById(location.hash.slice(1));
target && target.name && (target.checked = target.name.startsWith("__tabbed_"));
```

```APIDOC
A BungeeCord event which is called when an exception is raised in one of the coroutines managed by MCCoroutine. Cancelling this exception causes the error to not get logged and offers to possibility for custom logging.
```

### MCCoroutineExceptionEvent Usage

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/exception/index.html

Illustrates how to handle exceptions customly by listening to the MCCoroutineExceptionEvent. This event is dispatched to the Minecraft framework's event bus and can be used for custom logging or actions. It also mentions considerations for event handling, thread safety, and potential pitfalls.

```java
// Example of listening to MCCoroutineExceptionEvent (Bukkit)
Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvent(MCCoroutineExceptionEvent.class, new Listener() {
    @EventHandler
    public void onMCCoroutineException(MCCoroutineExceptionEvent event) {
        // Check if the event is for your plugin
        if (event.getPlugin().getName().equals("YourPluginName")) {
            // Handle the exception, e.g., log it differently
            System.err.println("Custom handling for: " + event.getCause().getMessage());
            // Optionally cancel the default logging
            // event.setCancelled(true);
        }
    }
}, ListenerPriority.NORMAL, YourPlugin.getInstance(), YourPlugin.getInstance());

// Considerations for suspend functions in event handlers:
// You can make this event a suspend function, however, put a try-catch over the entire function.
// Otherwise, any exception which occur while logging the original exception could stack indefinitely
// which eventually causes a OutOfMemoryException
```

### Theme Toggling Script

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/index.html

This JavaScript code handles theme toggling for the documentation. It checks local storage for user preferences and also respects the operating system's preferred color scheme. If a dark theme is preferred or saved, it applies the 'theme-dark' class to the HTML element.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode");
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches;
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage);
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark");
    }
}
```

### Minigame with Run Loop and Timer

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/tasks/index.html

Extends the `MiniGame` class with a `run` function that uses a `while` loop and `delay` to manage the game's remaining time. It continuously updates players on the game's status.

```kotlin
import kotlinx.coroutines.delay
import org.bukkit.entity.Player

class MiniGame {
    private var isStarted = false;
    private var players = HashSet<Player>()
    private var remainingTime = 0

    //...

    suspend fun start() {
        if (isStarted) {
            return
        }

        isStarted = true

        // This loop represents a traditional repeating task which ticks every 1 second and is called 20 times.
        for (i in 0 until 20) {
            sendMessageToPlayers("Game is starting in ${20 - i} seconds.")
            delay(1000)
        }

        // ... Teleport players to game.
        run()
    }

    private suspend fun run() {
        remainingTime = 300 // 300 seconds

        while (isStarted && remainingTime > 0) {
            sendMessageToPlayers("Game is over in ${remainingTime} seconds.")
            delay(1000)
            remainingTime--
        }

        if (!isStarted) {
            sendMessageToPlayers("Game was cancelled by external stop.")
        }

        isStarted = false
        // ... Teleport players back to lobby.
    }

    //...
}
```

```kotlin
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bungeecord.SuspendingPlugin

class MCCoroutineSamplePlugin : SuspendingPlugin() {
    override suspend fun onEnableAsync() {
        // BungeeCord Startup Thread
    }

    override suspend fun onDisableAsync() {
        // BungeeCord Shutdown Thread (Not the same as the startup thread)
    }
}
```

### Database Cache with Clear Functionality

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/caching/index.html

Demonstrates a `DatabaseCache` class in Kotlin for Bukkit plugins, featuring a `clear()` method to empty the cache and a `getDataFromPlayer()` suspend function to retrieve player data, utilizing `Deferred` and coroutine scopes for asynchronous operations.

```kotlin
import kotlinx.coroutines.*
import org.bukkit.entity.Player
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin

class DatabaseCache(private val database: Database, private val plugin: Plugin) {
    private val cache = HashMap<Player, Deferred<PlayerData>>()

    fun clear() {
        cache.clear()
    }

    suspend fun getDataFromPlayer(player: Player): PlayerData {
        return coroutineScope {
            if (!cache.containsKey(player)) {
                // Cache miss, create a new job
                cache[player] = async(Dispatchers.IO) {
                    database.getDataFromPlayer(player)
                }
            }

            // Await suspends the current context until the value of the ``Deferred`` job is ready.
            cache[player]!!.await()
        }
    }
}
```

### Initialize Dark Mode and JS Class

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.velocity/-m-c-coroutine/get-coroutine-session.html

This script initializes the dark mode preference based on local storage or OS settings and replaces the 'no-js' class with 'js' on the document element.

```javascript
var pathToRoot = "../../../";
document.documentElement.classList.replace("no-js","js");
const storage = localStorage.getItem("dokka-dark-mode")
if (storage == null) {
    const osDarkSchemePreferred = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
    if (osDarkSchemePreferred === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
} else {
    const savedDarkMode = JSON.parse(storage)
    if(savedDarkMode === true) {
        document.getElementsByTagName("html")[0].classList.add("theme-dark")
    }
}
```

--------------------------------

### Minecraft Coroutine Dispatcher (Bukkit)

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/ARTICLE.md

Implements a CoroutineDispatcher for the Bukkit primary thread. It dispatches tasks to the main thread if already on it, otherwise schedules them using Bukkit's server scheduler.

```kotlin
class MinecraftCoroutineDispatcher(private val plugin: Plugin) : CoroutineDispatcher() {
    /**
     * Handles dispatching the coroutine on the correct thread.
     */
    override fun dispatch(context: CoroutineContext, block: Runnable) {
        if (Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()) {
            block.run()
        } else {
            plugin.server.scheduler.runTask(plugin, block)
        }
    }
}
```

### MCCoroutine API Documentation

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/apidocs/mccoroutine-root/com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.velocity/-m-c-coroutine/index.html

Provides an interface for managing coroutine sessions within plugins. It includes functions to disable coroutines, disable logging, retrieve a coroutine session, and set up a coroutine session.

```APIDOC
MCCoroutine:
  interface

  Companion:
    object

  disable(plugin: Any):
    abstract fun disable(plugin: Any)
    - Disables coroutine for the given plugin.

  disableLogging(plugin: Any, suspendingPluginContainer: SuspendingPluginContainer):
    abstract fun disableLogging(plugin: Any, suspendingPluginContainer: SuspendingPluginContainer)
    - Disables logging false positives.

  getCoroutineSession(plugin: Any):
    abstract fun getCoroutineSession(plugin: Any): CoroutineSession
    - Get coroutine session for the given plugin.

  setupCoroutineSession(pluginInstance: Any, pluginContainer: Any, suspendingPluginContainer: SuspendingPluginContainer):
    abstract fun setupCoroutineSession(pluginInstance: Any, pluginContainer: Any, suspendingPluginContainer: SuspendingPluginContainer)
    - Configures the suspending plugin container with the real plugin Container.
```

### Repeating Tasks in Non-Suspend Functions (Kotlin)

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/docs/tasks.md

Demonstrates how to implement repeating tasks from non-suspend functions using `plugin.launch` and a loop with `delay`. This enables background repeating operations.

```kotlin
fun sayHello() {
    plugin.launch {
        println("Please say hello 10 times every 2 seconds")

        for (i in 0 until 10) {
            delay(2000) // Delay for 2000 milliseconds
            println("hello")
        }
    }
}
```

### Default onDisable Behavior with Coroutine Launch

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/docs/plugindisable.md

Demonstrates launching a coroutine in onDisable with the default shutdown strategy, which leads to a RuntimeException because the plugin is already disabled.

```kotlin
override fun onDisable() {
    println("[onDisable] Is starting on Thread:${Thread.currentThread().name}/${Thread.currentThread().id}/primaryThread=${Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()}")
    val plugin = this

    plugin.launch {
        println("[onDisable] Simulating data save on Thread:${Thread.currentThread().name}/${Thread.currentThread().id}/primaryThread=${Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()}")
        Thread.sleep(500)
    }

    println("[onDisable] Is ending on Thread:${Thread.currentThread().name}/${Thread.currentThread().id}/primaryThread=${Bukkit.isPrimaryThread()}")
}
```

### Bukkit Plugin with Suspending Executor and Events

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/site/commandexecutor/index.html

Demonstrates how to enable a Bukkit plugin using MCCoroutine. It registers suspending events and sets a suspending command executor for the 'playerdata' command. Requires 'playerdata' command declaration in plugin.yml.

```kotlin
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit.SuspendingJavaPlugin
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit.registerSuspendingEvents
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.bukkit.setSuspendingExecutor

class MCCoroutineSamplePlugin : SuspendingJavaPlugin() {
    private val database = Database()

    override suspend fun onEnableAsync() {
        // Minecraft Main Thread
        database.createDbIfNotExist()
        server.pluginManager.registerSuspendingEvents(PlayerDataListener(database), this)
        getCommand("playerdata")!!.setSuspendingExecutor(PlayerDataCommandExecutor(database))
    }

    override suspend fun onDisableAsync() {
        // Minecraft Main Thread
    }
}
```

### Velocity Coroutine Launch

Source: https://github.com/shynixn/mccoroutine/blob/master/docs/wiki/docs/coroutine.md

Launches a coroutine on the Velocity platform. This coroutine will execute on a random thread from the Velocity threadpool.

```kotlin
import com.github.shynixn.mccoroutine.velocity.launch
import com.velocitypowered.api.plugin.PluginContainer

fun foo(plugin: PluginContainer) {
    plugin.launch {
        // This will be a random thread on the Velocity threadpool
    }
}
```
